     管理層討論與分析



     2019 PERFORMANCE REVIEW                                                   2019年度業績回顧

     In 2019, despite the challenges of rising domestic and external risks,    2019 年，雖然國內外風險挑戰明顯上
     the national economy of China remained stable in overall operation,       升，但中國國民經濟運行總體依舊平
     while the quality of development improved steadily and major              穩，發展質量穩步提升，主要預期目標
     anticipated targets were achieved above expectation. Consumption          較好實現。消費作為經濟增長主動力作
     as a major driving force of economic growth was further reinforced,       用進一步鞏固，最終消費支出對國內生
     and the total expenditure of consumption contributed 57.8% to GDP         產總值增長的貢獻率為57.8%，高於資
     growth, 26.6 percentage points higher than the total amount of capital    本形成總額26.6個百分點。居民消費持
     formation. The consumption of residents continued to upgrade with         續升級提質。與此同時，線上消費佔社
     enhanced quality. Meanwhile, the percentage of online consumption in      會消費品零售總額比例進一步提升。在
     the total retail amount of private consumption increased further. Under   持續推進改革創新，深化供給側結構性
     the policies of continuous implementation of reforms and innovation,      改革，優化營商環境，大規模減稅降費
     deepening of supply-side structural reforms, improvement in business      等政策下，市場主體活力被持續激發。
     environment, and large-scale reduction in taxes and fees, the vitality    2019年，餐飲市場收入規模持續平穩增
     of market players was stimulated continuously. In 2019, the revenue       長，調味品行業總體亦持續趨勢向好。
     size of the catering market continued growing steadily, and the general

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES OF THE COMPANY                              本公司的企業管治常規

The Group is committed to achieving high standards of corporate            本集團致力達致高水準的企業管治，務

enhance corporate value and accountability.                                性。

contained in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules.

corporate governance and two levels of corporate governance                及兩個層面的企業管治常規如下：
practices, as follows:

(a)   code provisions, which listed issuers are expected to comply with    (a)   守則條文，期望上市發行人遵守或
      or to give considered reasons for deviation; and                           就偏離行為提供審慎考慮的理由；
                                                                                 及

(b)   recommended best practices for guidance only, which listed           (b)   建議最佳常規，僅屬指引，鼓勵上
      issuers are encouraged to comply with.                                     市發行人遵守。

The Company ’s corporate governance practices are based on                 本公司的企業管治常規乃以企業管治守
the principles and the code provisions as set out in the Corporate         則所載原則及守則條文為依據。
Governance Code.

e.   Principal Risk and Uncertainties                                     e.   主要風險及不確定性

     Risk of Product Quality                                                   產品質量風險

     The quality and safety of our products are very important to              產品的質量及安全對我們持續經營
     our continuous operation. If our quality control system is not            至關重要，一旦我們的質量控制體
     sufficiently implemented, quality or food safety issues may arise.        系出現控制不到位，則有可能出現
     With the increase of the number of OEM factories, if the OEM              質量或食品安全問題，且隨着OEM
     management is poor, there will be food safety risks and product           工廠數量的增加，若OEM管理不善
     quality risks, which will have a material adverse impact on the           會存在食品安全風險和產品質量風
     Company’s brand, reputation and financial condition.                      險，從而對公司的品牌、聲譽及財
                                                                               務狀況造成重大不利影響。

     Response Measures                                                         應對措施

     The Group has passed the HACCP System Certification and the               集團已經通過HACCP體系及
     ISO9001 System Certification. From the process of raw materials           ISO9001體系認證，自原料引入至
     import to products delivery and sales, strict implementation              產品出廠銷售均嚴格按照相關體系
     has been made in accordance with the requirements of such                 的要求執行，以確保產品品質。同
     systems to ensure product quality. At the same time, according            時，我們根據《食品安全風險評估
     to our “Food Safety Risk Assessment Criteria”, we will conduct            標準》，從法規風險、產品風險、運
     comprehensive risk assessments for food safety and quality under          營風險三個大的方面對食品安全與
     three major aspects: regulatory risk, product risk and operational        質量進行全方位的風險評估，通過
     risk. We will identify risk points through such risk assessments          風險評估識別風險點，並跟蹤評估
     and track and evaluate the effectiveness of risk rectification. In        風險整改的效果；針對OEM管理，
     respect of OEM management, we developed a quality control                 我們制定了代工廠質量管理制度，
     system for OEM factories by performing risk assessment on                 對不同代工廠進行風險評估並制定
     different OEM factories, formulating corresponding management             相應的管理措施，每季度進行質量
     measures and conducting quality audit quarterly in a bid to ensure        審核，以確保本集團運營風險可控。
     the Group’s operational risk is controllable.

     Risk of New Product Development                                           新產品開發風險

     As certain new product types belong to new industries, of which           由於部分新品類產品所屬行業較
     standards are not standardized and manufacturers are of vastly            新，行業標準不規範且生產商資質
     different qualities, the market environment for such new products         參差不齊，可能破壞新產品市場環
     may be damaged as a result and consumers may not trust such               境，導致消費者對新產品的不信
     products, thus affecting the Company in achieving its sales               賴，從而影響公司新產品銷售目標
     objectives for new products.                                              的實現。

Shareholders as a whole.

      Case: Traceable Tomatoes                                                       案例：可追溯的番茄

      The tomatoes used as raw materials by the Group can be traced                  本集團所使用的原料番茄從種子選擇到
      from seed selection to product delivery. At the planting stage, the            產品運輸可做到全程追溯。在種植階
      varieties of tomatoes and the selection of growers are determined after        段，番茄的品種及種植戶的選擇均由調
      evaluation, and all the pesticides used are collected by suppliers and         查後進行確認，所使用的農藥均由供應
      then distributed to growers. At each stage of tomato growth, quality           商集採分發給各個種植戶，在番茄生長
      control personnel will conduct spot check on farmers to verify whether         的各個階段均有品控人員對農戶進行點
      they have applied fertilizer, watered, weeded and other field work in          檢，核實其是否按照要求及配比進行施
      accordance with the requirements and proportion. At the harvesting             肥澆水、除草等田間工作。在採摘階
      stage, suppliers arrange the centralised machinery collections, and            段，供應商統一安排機器集採，中間不
      directly send the harvest to the detection area of the processing              經過任何組織及倉庫，直接送到加工廠
      factory to be tested for maturity and quality before going through any         待檢區域進行青果、霉果的檢測，檢測
      organisations and warehouses. After passing the test, tomatoes will be         合格將直接進行清洗投入生產，從採摘
      immediately cleaned and put into production. The whole process takes           到運輸投入生產不超過 4-6 個小時。在
      no more than 4-6 hours. For the whole production process of ketchup,           番茄醬的生產階段，全程由密閉管道加
      tomatoes are processed in closed pipelines and directly bottled and            工，直接進行灌裝封存，每桶番茄醬均
      sealed. Every barrel of ketchup has an identification number used for          有一個身份編號以便於追蹤，可追溯信
      tracing its information including specific production line, bottling nozzle,   息包括具體的生產線、灌裝噴嘴、運輸
      delivery vehicle, planting field and seed type.                                車輛、種植田地及種子型號。

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the          我們的目標，是對綜合財務報表整體是

towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this     本報告別無其他目的。我們不會就本報
report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a      告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with ISAs will always        何責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但
detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can             不能保證按照《國際審計準則》進行的審
arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or    計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發
in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence the         現。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，
economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated         如果合理預期它們單獨或匯總起來可能
financial statements.                                                        影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務

      2.    SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                               2.   重大會計政策概要（續）

            2.3 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting                         2.3 合併及權益會計原則

                  (i)    Subsidiaries                                                       (i)   附屬公司

                         Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured                      附屬公司指本集團擁有
                         entities) over which the Group has control. The Group                    控制權的所有實體（包括
                         controls an entity when the Group is exposed to, or                      結構性實體）。當本集團
                         has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with                透過參與實體業務而享
                         the entity and has the ability to affect those returns                   有或有權取得該實體的
                         through its power to direct the activities of the entity.                可變回報且有能力透過

          of the items.

          associated with the item will flow to the Group and the                 量時，才計入資產的賬面值

          to allocate their cost, net of their residual values, over              年期或（倘為租賃裝修及若干

                                                                                  價值），詳情如下：

          period.                                                                 適當時調整。

                  Construction-in-progress (“CIP”) represents buildings, plant               在建工程指在建或正在安裝的
                  and machinery under construction or pending for installation               建築、廠房及機器設備，並按
                  and is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses (if               成本減累計減值虧損（如有）

                  accordance with the policy as stated above.                                並根據上述政策進行折舊。

     2.10 Impairment of non-financial assets                                      2.10 非金融資產減值

          Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life are                  可使用年期不限定的無形資產
          not subject to amortisation and are tested annually for                    毋須攤銷，但每年須就減值進
          impairment, or more frequently if events or changes in                     行測試，或於發生事項或情況
          circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other                  出現變化而顯示可能出現減值
          assets are tested for impairment whenever events or                        時作出更頻密的減值測試。其
          changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount                 他資產會於發生事項或情況出
          may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                   現變化而顯示其賬面值未必能
          for the amount by which the asset ’s carrying amount                       夠收回時，進行減值測試。減
          exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount                     值虧損按有關資產賬面值超逾
          is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal              其可收回金額的數額確認。可
          and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment,                收回金額為資產的公平值減出
          assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which there                    售成本與使用價值兩者的較高
          are separately identifiable cash inflows which are largely                 者。就減值評估而言，資產按
          independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups                獨立可識別現金流入的最低
          of assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other              分類組合（現金產生單位）分
          than goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed                     類，其所產生的現金流入基本
          for possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each                 上獨立於其他資產或資產組
          reporting period.                                                          別。出現減值的非金融資產
                                                                                    （商譽除外）於各報告期末檢討
                                                                                     是否可能撥回減值。

                  Group’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate                 則應採用本集團的增量借款利
                  that the lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds                      率，即承租人為在類似經濟環
                  necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in a similar                 境下獲得價值相近的資產，以
                  economic environment with similar terms and conditions.                    類似條款和條件借入資金而必
                                                                                             須支付的利率。

                  To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                    為釐定增量借款利率，本集
                                                                                             團：

                  •      where possible, uses recent third-party financing                   •   在可能情況下，使用個
                         received by the individual lessee as a starting point,                  別承租人最近獲得的第
                         adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                     三方融資為出發點作出
                         since third party financing was received,                               調整以反映自獲得第三
                                                                                                 方融資以來融資條件的
                                                                                                 變動；

                  •      uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free               •   使用累加法，首先就本
                         interest rate adjusted for credit risk for leases held                  集團所持有租賃的信貸
                         by the Group, which does not have recent third party                    風險（最近並無第三方融
                         financing, and                                                          資）調整無風險利率；及

                  •      makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term,                 •   進行特定於租約的調
                         country, currency and security.                                         整，例如期限、國家、
                                                                                                 貨幣及抵押。

                  The Group is exposed to potential future increases in                      本集團未來可能根據指數或利
                  variable lease payments based on an index or rate, which                   率增加可變租賃付款額，而有
                  are not included in the lease liability until they take effect.            關指數或利率在生效前不會計
                  When adjustments to lease payments based on an index or                    入租賃負債。當根據指數或利
                  rate take effect, the lease liability is reassessed and adjusted           率對租賃付款作出的調整生效
                  against the right-of-use asset.                                            時，租賃負債會根據使用權資
                                                                                             產進行重新評估及調整。

      4.    CRITICAL ESTIMATES AND JUDGEMENTS                                        4.   重大估計及判斷

            The preparation of financial statements requires the use of                   編製財務報表需要使用會計估計，
            accounting estimates which, by definition, will seldom equal the              顧名思義，該等估計甚少符合實際
            actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgement in                結果。管理層亦須在應用本集團會
            applying the Group’s accounting policies.                                     計政策時作出判斷。

            Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are                  估計及判斷會予以持續評估，且基
            based on historical experience and other factors, including                   於歷史經驗及其他因素，包括對未
            expectations of future events that may have a financial impact                來事件的預期，而該等事件可能對
            on the entity and that are believed to be reasonable under the                實體造成財務影響且據信在某些情
            circumstances.                                                                況下屬合理。

                    terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain                     每份合同的租賃條款均單獨商

                    agreements do not impose any covenants, but leased assets                   及條件。租賃協議不包含契約
                    may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                         條款，但租賃資產不得用作借
                                                                                                款抵押。

                    property leases across the Group. These terms are used                      團若干物業租賃中。該等條款
                    to maximise operational flexibility in terms of managing                    用於提高管理合約的運作靈活
                    contracts. The majority of extension and termination options                性。所持有的大部分續租及終
                    held are exercisable only by the Group and not by the                       止選擇權僅可由本集團行使，

      34. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                               34. 關聯方交易

            Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability,          如一方有能力直接或間接控制另一
            directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise              方對另一方作出財務及經營決策行
            significant influence over the other party in making financial              使重大影響力，則各方被視為有關
            and operation decisions. Parties are also considered to be                  聯。如彼等受共同控制，各方亦被
            related if they are subject to common control. Members of key               視為有關聯。本集團主要管理層成
            management and their close family member of the Group are also              員及其近親亦被視為關聯方。
            considered as related parties.

            Save as disclosed elsewhere in this consolidated financial                  除本綜合財務資料其他部分所披露
            information, the Group has the following related transactions,              者外，本集團有以下關聯交易，該
            and they were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s             等交易於本集團日常業務過程中進
            business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.                行且基於相互協定的條款釐定。
